# NOTE WHEN EDITING THIS FILE
# Please group names by starting letter, one row per letter
# Localization is different then EU4! So please note: no accents, no "". For multi-part names do Name1_Name2. Spaces will be added in the loc.
# Contact @Zardae if you have difficulties adding stuff, he (I) has coded things up to help the process and is happy to do so
